<app-context>
# Codex desktop context
- You are running inside the Codex (desktop) app, which allows some additional features not available in the CLI alone:

### Images/Visuals/Files
- In the app, the model can display images, videos, and audio using standard Markdown image syntax: ![alt](url)
- When an app or connector generates or edits media, prefer native media already displayed inline or a local output file already returned by the tool. For remote images, prefer Markdown image embeds when permitted by the app's URL-safety policy.
- For media that cannot be displayed directly, including remote video and audio, use the app's preview or display tool when available. Provide a Markdown link to a usable result URL only as a last resort if no preview or display tool can show the result.
- Do not download remote media to work around display restrictions.
- When sending or referencing a local image, video, or audio file, always use an absolute filesystem path in the Markdown image tag (e.g., ![alt](/absolute/path.png)); relative paths and plain text will not render the media.
- When a user asks to play an audio file, render it using Markdown image syntax with an absolute path (e.g., ![audio](/absolute/path.mp3)).
- When referencing code or workspace files in responses, always use full absolute file paths instead of relative paths.
- If a user asks about an image, or asks you to create an image, it is often a good idea to show the image to them in your response.
- Return web URLs as Markdown links (e.g., [label](https://example.com)).

### Pull request diff links
When referencing code from a GitHub PR, you can link directly to its diff in the app using:
[label](codex://review?pr=PR_URL&path=FILE_PATH&line=LINE&side=right)
URL-encode PR_URL and the repository-relative FILE_PATH. Use a verified one-based LINE from the current PR diff. Use side=left for the original code or side=right for the updated code. Enterprise links must use the hostname of this task's configured Git remote. Use ordinary file links for workspace code.

### Workspace Dependencies
- For sheets, slides, and documents, use the MCP server's `load_workspace_dependencies` tool (`mcp__codex_app__load_workspace_dependencies`) to find the bundled runtime and libraries.

### Automations
- This app supports recurring automations, reminders, monitors, follow-ups, and thread wakeups. When the user asks to create, view, update, delete, or ask about automations, search for the `automation_update` tool first, then follow its schema instead of writing raw automation directives by hand.
- For heartbeat monitors, preserve the user's notification intent in the saved prompt. Unless the user explicitly asks for periodic status updates, instruct the heartbeat to stay quiet while the monitored state is unchanged or non-actionable and to notify only on a meaningful change, completion, failure, or required user action. Do not add instructions such as "leave a brief status update" on every run.
- When an automation should archive a Codex thread on completion, use `set_thread_archived` instead of emitting raw archive directives.

### Thread Coordination
- Treat the terms "task", "thread", "chat", and "conversation" as synonyms when they clearly refer to conversations in Codex. Use "chat" when referring to conversations in the product. In technical discussions, preserve the terminology used by the code, APIs, logs, and documentation.
- When the user asks to create, fork, inspect, continue, hand off, pin, archive, unarchive, rename, or otherwise manage Codex threads, search for the relevant thread tool first: `create_thread`, `fork_thread`, `list_threads`, `list_archived_threads`, `read_thread`, `wait_threads`, `send_message_to_thread`, `handoff_thread`, `set_thread_pinned`, `set_thread_archived`, or `set_thread_title`.
- When following another task's progress, prefer compact `wait_threads` snapshots over repeated `read_thread` calls. Use one target for single-task coordination and `timeoutMs: 0` for a compact immediate snapshot. `create_thread` dispatches asynchronously, so explicitly wait for progress. Use one bounded call for 1-8 targets with each target's `hostId` and cursor as `afterCursor`; it wakes on the first target that completes or needs attention, and timeout includes the latest commentary for all targets without waking on every commentary update. An up-to-date cursor suppresses already-delivered final text. Separate waits from one task may run serially. Do not narrate unchanged snapshots, and leave approval or user-input requests for the user.
- Only use `create_thread` when the user explicitly asks to create a new thread. Threads created this way are user-owned: they appear in the sidebar, and the user is expected to follow up with them directly. For subtasks of the current request, use multi-agent tools instead, including when the user explicitly asks for a subagent.
- After a successful `create_thread` call, emit `::created-thread{threadId="..."}` for a created thread or `::created-thread{clientThreadId="..."}` for queued worktree setup on its own line in your final response.

### Worktrees
- Prefer reusing a suitable active worktree. Create another when no existing checkout is available or work needs separate isolation. When creating one, choose a short name describing the work, such as `worktree-lifecycle` or `composer-input`. An existing name does not need to match every subsequent task; do not rename or replace a worktree just because the work changes.
- Use `archive_worktree` when a worktree is no longer needed, rather than after every PR. Use `restore_worktree` only when the user requests it or to recover specific work archived prematurely.
- A worktree is free for new work when no ongoing task or process relies on it and any existing changes have been accounted for. Prepare the appropriate branch and base before starting new work. Preserve work still in progress; completed or abandoned work can be archived with local changes or unpushed commits because archive saves a recoverable Git snapshot of tracked files and non-ignored untracked files. Ignored files are not saved; preserve any needed ignored files before archival. Do not delete files just to make a worktree eligible for archival. Preserve pinned, shared, or in-use worktrees when cleaning up. Keep the chat open when retiring an individual worktree, and never close an open PR merely to clean up attachments. Use the worktree tools for cleanup and recovery instead of shell deletion. Check at these lifecycle transitions, not every turn.
- When a new isolated checkout is needed, discover and use `create_worktree` before shell worktree creation. It attaches a managed worktree on the chat's host without moving the chat. Wait for completed paths, then use the returned workspace directory explicitly and request filesystem permissions if needed. Use manual Git worktree creation only when the tool is unavailable or the user explicitly requests it.

### Inline Code Comments
- Use the ::code-comment{...} directive when you need to attach feedback directly to specific code lines.
- Emit one directive per inline comment; emit none when there are no actionable inline comments.
- Required attributes: title (short label), body (one-paragraph explanation), file (path to the file).
- Optional attributes: start, end (1-based line numbers), priority (0-3).
- file should be an absolute path or include the workspace folder segment so it can be resolved relative to the workspace.
- Keep line ranges tight; end defaults to start.
- Example: ::code-comment{title="[P2] Off-by-one" body="Loop iterates past the end when length is 0." file="/path/to/foo.ts" start=10 end=11 priority=2}

### Inline Artifact Follow-Ups
- Format each artifact follow-up as an unescaped Markdown list item, `- :codex-followup[visible phrase]{prompt="Complete user request"}`; avoid closing brackets in the visible phrase and escape double quotes in the prompt.

### Git
- Branch prefix: `codex/`. Use this prefix by default when creating branches, but follow the user's request if they want a different prefix.
</app-context>

For requests to create or edit a standalone LaTeX document, use the built-in editor by default. Create or edit the .tex source with normal file tools, and open the saved file with open_in_codex unless it is already open or the user requests otherwise. Keep follow-up edits in that same file and editor. Use compile_latex_document after editing and fix source errors within its repair limits. Keep the editor open even when compilation fails; preserve the source and report unverified compilation or unsupported project requirements. Discover these tools if deferred. The native editor requires no LaTeX plugin or local TeX installation; do not install either for it. Ordinary math explanations stay in chat.

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

*Written 2026-09-07 from observed behavior. Correct it whenever it's wrong — that is the
point of the file.*

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — unprocessed only; `ingest` moves them to `raw/sources/clips/` when done |
| Application code | `projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **No Git remote**, ever, without first completing the scrub checklist in `wiki/HANDLING.md`. The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex runs the catalog minus the wiki/Obsidian and `graphify` skills, so the two skill tables legitimately differ.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

The current Teacher Workspace has a Payments panel, but `/classroom` still shows you the student Home. This round defines what the teacher Home should do.

❓ **Q1 - First thing you see:** When you open the dashboard, what should lead: **what needs your action**, **today’s teaching schedule**, or **student progress**?

➡️ **My recommendation:** Lead with “Needs your attention,” then today’s sessions. Put progress and recent activity below so urgent work is visible without hiding the bigger picture.

---

❓ **Q2 - Student work:** When you say “all the tasks they have to do,” do you mean **assignments students owe**, **your follow-ups for each student**, or both? What would you want to do from that section?

➡️ **My recommendation:** Show both, clearly separated. Each item should open the student or assignment, and your follow-ups should have a way to mark them done.

---

❓ **Q3 - Performance at a glance:** Which student changes should reach your Home: **low activity**, **missed assignments**, **accuracy changes**, **recent test results**, or something else? Should the Home show individual students who need attention, an overall class view, or both?

➡️ **My recommendation:** Show a short list of students who may need attention, with a concrete reason and a link to their full analytics. Keep detailed charts on the student’s analytics page.

---

❓ **Q4 - New activity:** Which events deserve an alert that stays until you handle it? For example: a new signup, request to work with you, unread message, payment needing a student match, refund, or completed assignment.

➡️ **My recommendation:** Keep actionable alerts for requests, unread messages, payment exceptions, and work that needs your response. Put routine completions and ordinary payments in a recent activity feed.

---

❓ **Q5 - Visual direction:** Of the six images, which one feels closest to the dashboard you want? You can also name parts you like, such as image 2’s clear layout, image 3’s compact schedule, or image 6’s warmer cards.

➡️ **My recommendation:** Use image 2 as the layout reference, with some of image 6’s warmth. The charts should reflect real teaching decisions rather than fill space.

See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\r\n# CLAUDE.md\r\n\r\nThis file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.\r\n\r\n## Project snapshot\r\n\r\nLSAT U is a premium ed-tech web app: React 18 + Vite 5 + TypeScript + Tailwind + shadcn/ui, Supabase backend with Edge Functions, hosted on Lovable. The Lovable-connected project is the source of truth; GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`) is the mirror. Treat the connected project as canonical unless told otherwise.\r\n\r\n## Second brain (product memory)\r\n\r\nProduct context, design decisions, pedagogy, and status do NOT live in this repo. They live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`. This repo is the code; the vault is the decision layer. Read the vault when starting a session, needing product/design/pedagogy context, or making a decision that touches roadmap or UX. Skip it for trivial code edits.\r\n\r\nPath sanity check first:\r\n\r\n- Vault root (the `~/ai-os` directory IS the Obsidian vault): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os`\r\n- Code repo root (this project): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/projects/lsatu`\r\n\r\nIf any older note says `~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/...` or `~/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/LSATU`, treat that as stale — the vault was consolidated into `~/ai-os`. Use the absolute paths above.\r\n\r\nProtocol is canonical in `~/ai-os/CLAUDE.md` (Session Start / Session End), run via two skills:\r\n\r\n- **`/pickup`** (start / "brief me"): reads `wiki/tasks.md` → `wiki/hot.md` → `wiki/dashboard.md` → `wiki/index.md` → latest `wiki/log.md` entry, then gives a 6-8 line briefing (Now / Next / open threads / suggested next) and waits.\r\n- **`/wrapup`** (handoff / "done for today"): proposes which tasks are done (confirm first), strikes them through in `wiki/tasks.md`, refreshes `wiki/hot.md`, appends a dated `wiki/log.md` entry, updates the task anchor, gives a 4-line confirmation.\r\n\r\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the living ledger of Now / Next / Done; finished work is struck through with a date so a new session never re-runs it.\r\n\r\nWhere LSATU truth lives in the vault (paths relative to `~/ai-os`):\r\n\r\nThe vault was rebuilt on 2026-07-16. **Live** pages:\r\n\r\n- Teaching voice (route ALL student-facing copy through it): `wiki/teaching-voice.md`\r\n- The tutoring business (offers, channels, priorities): `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`\r\n- Hubs: `wiki/tasks.md`, `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/log.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, `wiki/index.md`\r\n- A student\'s whole record (transcripts, notes, homework): `students/<name>/` — see `students/README.md` for the primary/mirror rule\r\n- LSAT teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md` (fills by ingest)\r\n\r\n**Archived** — the pre-rebuild product knowledge is read-only reference at `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`; it is not maintained and may be stale. Re-ingest a page through `raw/` if it becomes load-bearing again rather than editing it in place:\r\n\r\n- Product context: `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md` (flagship Main Conclusion bootcamp)\r\n- Design system (colors, type, role tokens): `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`\r\n- Code/source map: `…/synthesis/lsatu-source-map.md` (regenerable from this codebase)\r\n- Quality bar all bootcamps finish to: `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`\r\n- Concepts, entities, domain index: `…/concepts/`, `…/entities/`, `…/index.md`\r\n\r\nRules: `raw/` is immutable input, never edit it. Read knowledge from `wiki/`, not `raw/`. Use the `obsidian` CLI (`search`, `backlinks`, `read`) for graph traversal (needs the Obsidian app open); flat file reads work without it. When saving deliverables to the vault, use `[[wiki-link]]` syntax and `UPPERCASE-HYPHENATED.md` naming.\r\n\r\n## Commands\r\n\r\nScripts (from `package.json`):\r\n\r\n- `npm run dev` — Vite dev server on port 8080\r\n- `npm run build` — production build\r\n- `npm run build:dev` — build with development mode flags\r\n- `npm run preview` — serve the built bundle\r\n- `npm run lint` — ESLint flat config (`eslint.config.js`)\r\n- `npm run test` — Vitest single run (runner wired, no specs exist yet)\r\n- `npm run test:watch` — Vitest watch mode\r\n- Single test file: `npx vitest run path/to/file.test.ts`\r\n\r\nPackage manager: **npm**. `package-lock.json` is canonical. A stray `bun.lock` / `bun.lockb` exists in the repo — ignore it, do not switch tooling.\r\n\r\n- `npm run typecheck` — `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`. Not optional before a push: `vite build` uses esbuild, which strips types without checking them, so a type error ships and auto-deploys. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on this.\r\n- `npm run test:e2e` / `npm run playwright` — Playwright e2e (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\r\n\r\nNo Prettier. Formatting is ESLint-only.\r\n\r\n## Architecture (big picture)\r\n\r\n- **Routing** — React Router v6. Routes are registered in `src/App.tsx`, pages live in `src/pages/`. `/foyer` is the landing surface. Feature-gated pages are wrapped by `ProtectedRoute`.\r\n- **Auth + permissions** — Supabase Auth session plus Lovable Cloud Auth JWT fallback (`@lovable.dev/cloud-auth-js`). Per-feature access flags (`has_practice_access`, `has_drill_access`, `has_chat_access`, `is_admin`, ...) are resolved in `src/hooks/useUserPermissions.ts`. Session death is detected via JWT/401.\r\n- **Supabase integration** — client auto-generated at `src/integrations/supabase/client.ts` (Supabase JS v2). DB types are auto-generated — do not hand-edit. Edge Functions (Deno) in `supabase/functions/`: `admin-manage-users`, `analyze-stem-response`, `generate-micro-drill`, `motivation-engine`, `parse-drill-request`, `tutor-chat`, `voice-coach-respond`. SQL migrations in `supabase/migrations/`.\r\n- **State** — React Context for cross-cutting concerns (`AuthContext`, `UserSettingsContext`, `QuestionBankContext`, `ThemeContext`, `TimerContext`). TanStack Query v5 for server state.\r\n- **Primary navigation — Foyer** — `src/pages/AcademyFoyer.tsx` composes `src/components/foyer/FoyerSidebar.tsx` (state blocks + workspace nav) with `src/components/foyer/FoyerHeroRing.tsx` (3 nodes: Smart Drill, Resume, Ask Joshua). This is the primary nav for the product.\r\n- **Messaging / Inbox** — real messaging feature, not cosmetic. Lives in `src/components/inbox/` (`FloatingMessenger`, `ConversationView`, `ThreadList`) plus `src/hooks/useInbox.ts`. One ongoing 1:1 thread per student with the admin (Joshua). Supports PDF attachments. Gated by `has_chat_access`.\r\n- **Practice surfaces** — Drill and Wrong Answer Journal (WAJ) are real product surfaces, not launchers. Core logic in `src/lib/`: `adaptiveEngine`, `drillIntelligence`, `achievementEngine`, `gamification`, `questionLoader`, `streakSystem`, `wajService`.\r\n- **Admin** — `AdminDashboard` + `admin-manage-users` edge function. Must be desktop AND mobile responsive. Needs granular per-feature toggle control.\r\n- **UI primitives** — shadcn/ui (Radix + `class-variance-authority`) in `src/components/ui/`. Icons from `lucide-react` and `@tabler/icons-react`. Framer Motion for animation. Spline for 3D.\r\n- **Styling** — Tailwind 3.4 over HSL custom properties in `src/index.css`, surfaced as tokens in `tailwind.config.ts`. `DESIGN.md` is the visual authority; `.impeccable/design.json` is its machine-readable sidecar. Prefer tokens over ad-hoc hex values, and never add a token name whose CSS variable is not defined - three such names (`accent-bronze`, `accent-warm`, `glow-bronze`) shipped broken for five months and silently removed the focus ring from every shared Button.\r\n\r\n## Conventions\r\n\r\n- **Git workflow:** Always commit and push directly to `main`. Never create feature branches or pull requests unless explicitly asked. After completing any code change: stage relevant files, commit with a descriptive message, and push to `main`.\r\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session; a raw push can get rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges origin/main if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes.\r\n- Path alias `@/` → `./src/` (set in `vite.config.ts` and `tsconfig.json`).\r\n- TypeScript strict mode is **off** (`strictNullChecks: false`, `noImplicitAny: false`, `noUnusedLocals: false`). Do not flip these without explicit approval — it would cascade into a very large diff.\r\n- Lovable-specific plumbing that must not be removed:\r\n  - `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin injects `data-lov-id` in dev\r\n  - `vite-plugin-pwa` configured with start URL `/foyer`\r\n  - Lovable preview Service Worker guard in `src/main.tsx`\r\n- Identity rules: email sign-in, profile identity uses **name** (not username), instructor is displayed as **"Joshua"**, students display their real profile names.\r\n\r\n## Cross-tool instruction hygiene\r\n\r\n`CLAUDE.md` is the fuller project source of truth. `AGENTS.md` is the Codex-facing adapter and should stay aligned without becoming a second full copy.\r\n\r\n- When updating project instructions, update both files as needed: put durable detail here, and keep `AGENTS.md` as a concise adapter.\r\n- When creating a Claude skill that Codex should use, also copy it to `.agents/skills/` with its `SKILL.md` and supporting files.\r\n- When creating a Claude agent that Codex should spawn, also create a matching `.codex/agents/<name>.toml` and place the converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\r\n- When adding shared knowledge, put it in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/` so every tool can read the same source.\r\n\r\n## Verification (must not skip)\r\n\r\nAny change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not "done" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + browser tool, e.g. Playwright or chrome devtools MCP), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Do not report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This has been the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions (repeated "it\'s not working" / "i dont see it" corrections) — treat it as a hard gate, not a suggestion.\r\n\r\n**Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** That means narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A responsive defect that gets pushed as "fixed" and does not hold costs a second full session — this has already happened (a pushed Home-page fix that did not survive, then 13 screenshots and 8 emulate cycles to actually close it). One matrix pass before the push is cheaper than either.\r\n\r\n## Product rules (must not regress)\r\n\r\n- Foyer (`FoyerSidebar` + `FoyerHeroRing`) is the primary navigation. The Foyer additionally hosts a `GooeyDock` quick-launch row well below the orbital ring; this dock is allowed only on `/foyer`. Do not add docks elsewhere.\r\n- Inbox is real messaging with back-and-forth threads and PDF attachments, scoped 1:1 per student with admin.\r\n- Admin is fully responsive on desktop and mobile.\r\n- Do not expose actions users cannot actually perform — no fake analytics, no dead buttons, no misleading placeholders. Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake data.\r\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email.\r\n\r\n## Known gaps (do not invent answers)\r\n\r\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run them with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\r\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows. Nothing runs `typecheck`, `lint`, or the tests automatically — `.claude/safe-push.sh` is the only gate, and only for typecheck.\r\n- `README.md` is a stub.\r\n- `.claude/settings.json` is set up with a permission allowlist for safe dev work (npm scripts, git reads, MCP GitHub reads) and a denylist for destructive ops. No custom `.claude/agents/`. A `SessionStart` auto-pull hook is wired in `.claude/settings.local.json` (runs `.claude/auto-pull-hook.sh`); Codex mirrors it in `.codex/hooks.json`.\r\n\r\n## Skill / agent / command vault\r\n\r\nThe full skill/agent/command vault lives in the brain root at `~/ai-os/skills/`, `~/ai-os/agents/`, `~/ai-os/commands/` (split into `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees, indexed by `~/ai-os/skills/INDEX.md`). It is inert storage — nothing here auto-loads. To use a vault skill in this project, symlink it into `.claude/skills/` (Codex: `.agents/skills/`). When you create a new skill/agent/command, save it to BOTH this project\'s `.claude/<kind>/` and the vault, so the vault stays the complete catalog.\r\n\r\n## Agent skills\r\n\r\n### Issue tracker\r\n\r\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\r\n\r\n### Triage labels\r\n\r\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\r\n\r\n### Domain docs\r\n\r\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\r\n'}]

Fold it onto the Inbox room, dropping only `pane`\\r\\n * so the thread and every other param survive. This is the one place that\\r\\n * knows about the legacy shape; no surface reads `?ws=` any more.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function teacherRoomPathForSource(path: string) {\\r\\n  if (!path.startsWith(\'/inbox\')) return path;\\r\\n  const url = new URL(path, \'http://lsatu.local\');\\r\\n  const next = new URLSearchParams();\\r\\n  url.searchParams.forEach((value, key) => {\\r\\n    if (key === \'pane\') return;\\r\\n    next.set(key, value);\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  const query = next.toString();\\r\\n  return `/teacher/inbox${query ? `?${query}` : \'\'}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Today: the work queue, not a chat window (spec 5.1). The queue is the main\\r\\n * column; today\'s sessions and the counts sit above it.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Every number here is counted off the queue that is rendered directly below\\r\\n * it, so a count can never claim work the list does not show. While the\\r\\n * sources are still loading the counts are withheld rather than shown as zero.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function DeskTodaySurface() {\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const [chatInsert, setChatInsert] = useState<DeskChatInsert | null>(null);\\r\\n  const { localPreview, todoQueue, isLoading } = useDeskTaskQueue();\\r\\n\\r\\n  const items = todoQueue.items;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const todaySessions = useMemo(\\r\\n    () =>\\r\\n      items.filter(\\r\\n        (item): item is CalendarTodoItem =>\\r\\n          item.sourceKind === \'calendar\' && isSameDay(parseISO(item.startAt), new Date()),\\r\\n      ),\\r\\n    [items],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  const counts = useMemo(\\r\\n    () => ({\\r\\n      email: items.filter((item) => item.priorityBand === \'email\').length,\\r\\n      calendar: items.filter((item) => item.priorityBand === \'calendar\').length,\\r\\n      work: items.filter((item) => item.priorityBand === \'work\').length,\\r\\n    }),\\r\\n    [items],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  // A plain stack, not a flex-1 column: the queue sizes to its own content and\\r\\n  // `main` does the scrolling. Letting the sections flex-shrink collapsed the\\r\\n  // list and painted its cards over the section below it.\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"flex flex-col gap-6 px-5 py-6 sm:px-6\\">\\r\\n      <SignupQueue />\\r\\n\\r\\n      <PaymentsPanel />\\r\\n\\r\\n      <section className=\\"grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_260px]\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"border border-black/12 bg-white/25 px-4 py-3.5\\">\\r\\n          <div className=\\"mb-2.5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/40\\">\\r\\n            Today&rsquo;s sessions\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n          {isLoading ? (\\r\\n            <p className=\\"text-[13px] text-black/45\\">Loading</p>\\r\\n          ) : todaySessions.length === 0 ? It moves into the shell\'s AI panel in\\r\\n          Phase 3; until then Today keeps the composer so the queue\'s\\r\\n          \\"draft this\\" action still has somewhere to land. */}\\r\\n      <section className=\\"flex h-[520px] shrink-0 flex-col border-t border-black/10 pt-5\\">\\r\\n        <DeskChatPanel\\r\\n          localPreview={localPreview}\\r\\n          insert={chatInsert}\\r\\n          onInsertConsumed={() => setChatInsert(null)}\\r\\n        />\\r\\n      </section>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"q":1,"result":"import { Plus } from \'lucide-react\';\\r\\nimport HubAssignments from \'@/components/hub/HubAssignments\';\\r\\nimport { BootcampLessonProgress } from \'@/components/teacher/bootcamps/BootcampLessonProgress\';\\r\\nimport { StudentIndicatorLedger } from \'@/features/activity-centre/components/StudentIndicatorLedger\';\\r\\nimport { useDossier } from \'./StudentDossier\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Work = every assignment kind for this student in one place.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * This reuses `HubAssignments`, which already queries all three real sources\\r\\n * (`homework_assignments`, `ta_assignments`, `classroom_items`) and carries\\r\\n * the unassign/delete flows. It groups by kind rather than interleaving one\\r\\n * newest-first list; each group is already newest-first. Rebuilding it as a\\r\\n * single interleaved timeline would have meant re-deriving those queries and\\r\\n * dropping the delete affordances, so the grouping stands until there is a\\r\\n * reason to trade that away.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function DossierWork() {\\r\\n  const { studentId, openAssign } = useDossier();\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"space-y-4 p-5\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"flex justify-end\\">\\r\\n        <button\\r\\n          type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n          onClick={openAssign}\\r\\n          className=\\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-teacher-accent px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-teacher-accent-hover\\"\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <Plus className=\\"h-3.5 w-3.5\\" strokeWidth={2} />\\r\\n          Assign work\\r\\n        </button>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <HubAssignments studentId={studentId} />\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* Renders nothing when there is no stored progress, so it never adds an\\r\\n          empty block. */}\\r\\n      <BootcampLessonProgress studentClassId={studentId} />\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* What their Activity work says to teach next, read from the attempts\\r\\n          they have already made. Also renders nothing when there are none. */}\\r\\n      <StudentIndicatorLedger studentClassId={studentId} />\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"q":2,"result":"/**\\r\\n * Everything a student has actually done, on the teacher\'s side of the glass.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The data was always there — every sitting writes a `section_history` row\\r\\n * with its whole per-question record, and every day of practice writes a\\r\\n * `daily_stats` row — but nothing outside the student\'s own account ever read\\r\\n * it. This is that reader, keyed on class_id because that is how the teacher\\r\\n * workspace knows a student.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * One rule governs the empty state: reading these rows depends on the admin\\r\\n * SELECT policy, and a blocked read comes back as an empty list rather than an\\r\\n * error. So \\"no rows\\" is never rendered as \\"this student has done nothing\\" —\\r\\n * it says what it actually knows, which is that nothing came back.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { useQuery } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\\r\\nimport { Loader2 } from \'lucide-react\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { fetchSectionHistoryByClass, summarizeSections } from \'@/lib/sectionHistory\';\\r\\nimport SectionHistoryList from \'@/components/history/SectionHistoryList\';\\r\\nimport { formatDurationMs } from \'@/lib/drillHistory\';\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface DailyStat {\\r\\n  date: string;\\r\\n  questions_answered: number | null;\\r\\n  correct_answers: number | null;\\r\\n  time_spent_ms: number | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Days of usage to show. A term\'s worth, which is what a session prep needs. */\\r\\nconst USAGE_DAYS = 60;\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction Tile({ label, value, sub }: { label: string; value: string; sub?: string }) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"rounded-xl border border-black/10 bg-white/50 p-4\\">\\r\\n      <p className=\\"text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-black/45\\">{label}</p>\\r\\n      <p className=\\"mt-1 text-xl font-semibold text-black/85\\">{value}</p>\\r\\n      {sub && <p className=\\"mt-0.5 text-xs text-black/45\\">{sub}</p>}\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function StudentWorkPanel({ classId }: { classId: string }) {\\r\\n  const sections = useQuery({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'teacher-student-sections\', classId],\\r\\n    enabled: !!classId,\\r\\n    queryFn: () => fetchSectionHistoryByClass(classId),\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const usage = useQuery({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'teacher-student-usage\', classId],\\r\\n    enabled: !!classId,\\r\\n    queryFn: async (): Promise<DailyStat[]> => {\\r\\n      const since = new Date();\\r\\n      since.setDate(since.getDate() - USAGE_DAYS);\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'daily_stats\')\\r\\n        .select(\'date, questions_answered, correct_answers, time_spent_ms\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'class_id\', classId)\\r\\n        .gte(\'date\', since.toISOString().slice(0, 10))\\r\\n        .order(\'date\', { ascending: false });\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      return (data ?? []) as DailyStat[];\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (sections.isLoading || usage.isLoading) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <div className=\\"flex items-center gap-2 py-8 text-sm text-black/45\\">\\r\\n        <Loader2 className=\\"h-4 w-4 animate-spin\\" />\\r\\n        Reading their work…\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (sections.isError || usage.isError) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <p className=\\"py-8 text-sm text-black/55\\">\\r\\n        Their work could not be read. This is usually the admin read policy not being\\r\\n        applied to this database yet, rather than an empty record.\\r\\n      </p>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const rows = sections.data ?? [];\\r\\n  const days = usage.data ?? [];\\r\\n  const summary = summarizeSections(rows);\\r\\n  const totalMs = days.reduce((sum, d) => sum + (d.time_spent_ms ?? 0), 0);\\r\\n  const totalQuestions = days.reduce((sum, d) => sum + (d.questions_answered ?? 0), 0);\\r\\n  const activeDays = days.filter((d) => (d.questions_answered ?? 0) > 0).length;\\r\\n  const lastActive = days.find((d) => (d.questions_answered ?? 0) > 0)?.date ?? null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"space-y-6\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4\\">\\r\\n        <Tile\\r\\n          label=\\"Sections sat\\"\\r\\n          value={String(summary.sections)}\\r\\n          sub={summary.bestLabel ? `Best ${summary.bestPercent}% · ${summary.bestLabel}` : undefined}\\r\\n        />\\r\\n        <Tile\\r\\n          label={`Time on task · ${USAGE_DAYS}d`}\\r\\n          value={formatDurationMs(totalMs)}\\r\\n          sub={`${activeDays} day${activeDays === 1 ? \'\' : \'s\'} worked`}\\r\\n        />\\r\\n        <Tile label=\\"Questions answered\\" value={String(totalQuestions)} sub={`last ${USAGE_DAYS} days`} />\\r\\n        <Tile\\r\\n          label=\\"Last worked\\"\\r\\n          value={lastActive ? new Date(`${lastActive}T00:00:00`).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {\\r\\n            month: \'short\',\\r\\n            day: \'numeric\',\\r\\n          }) : \'—\'}\\r\\n          sub={lastActive ? undefined : \'no practice recorded\'}\\r\\n        />\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      {/* Day by day, so \\"how long were they on and when\\" is answerable rather\\r\\n          than averaged away. Only days with work: a row of zeroes for every\\r\\n          day they did nothing is noise, and the count above already says how\\r\\n          many days were worked. */}\\r\\n      {days.some((d) => (d.questions_answered ?? 0) > 0) && (\\r\\n        <div className=\\"rounded-xl border border-black/10 bg-white/40\\">\\r\\n          <div className=\\"border-b border-black/10 px-4 py-2 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-black/45\\">\\r\\n            Usage by day\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n          <div className=\\"max-h-64 divide-y divide-black/5 overflow-y-auto\\">\\r\\n            {days\\r\\n              .filter((d) => (d.questions_answered ?? 0) > 0)\\r\\n              .map((d) => (\\r\\n                <div key={d.date} className=\\"flex items-center gap-4 px-4 py-2 text-sm\\">\\r\\n                  <span className=\\"w-28 shrink-0 text-black/70\\">\\r\\n                    {new Date(`${d.date}T00:00:00`).toLocaleDateString(undefined, {\\r\\n                      weekday: \'short\',\\r\\n                      month: \'short\',\\r\\n                      day: \'numeric\',\\r\\n                    })}\\r\\n                  </span>\\r\\n                  <span className=\\"w-20 tabular-nums text-black/55\\">\\r\\n                    {formatDurationMs(d.time_spent_ms ?? 0)}\\r\\n                  </span>\\r\\n                  <span className=\\"tabular-nums text-black/55\\">\\r\\n                    {d.correct_answers ?? 0}/{d.questions_answered ?? 0} correct\\r\\n                  </span>\\r\\n                </div>\\r\\n              ))}\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n\\r\\n      <div>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mb-2 text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-black/45\\">\\r\\n          Sections sat\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        {rows.length === 0 ? (\\r\\n          <p className=\\"text-sm text-black/55\\">\\r\\n            No sittings came back for this student. Either they have not sat a section yet,\\r\\n            or the admin read policy is not applied to this database.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        ) : (\\r\\n          <SectionHistoryList rows={rows} />\\r\\n        )}\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"q":3,"result":"Import-Clixml: Unexpected end of file while parsing Name has occurred. Replaces the split between TeacherStudentDetail, the\\r\\n * StudentHub page, and the roster\'s inline expander.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface DossierContext {\\r\\n  studentId: string;\\r\\n  student: HubStudentRow | undefined;\\r\\n  studentName: string | null;\\r\\n  studentEmail: string | null;\\r\\n  /** Opens the shared assign flow already scoped to this student. */\\r\\n  openAssign: () => void;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function useDossier() {\\r\\n  return useOutletContext<DossierContext>();\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst TABS = [\\r\\n  { to: \'overview\', label: \'Overview\' },\\r\\n  { to: \'work\', label: \'Work\' },\\r\\n  { to: \'notes\', label: \'Notes\' },\\r\\n  { to: \'messages\', label: \'Messages\' },\\r\\n  { to: \'schedule\', label: \'Schedule\' },\\r\\n] as const;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function StudentDossier() {\\r\\n  const { studentId } = useParams<{ studentId: string }>();\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n\\r\\n  const student = useSelectedHubStudent(studentId ?? null);\\r\\n  // The roster query may not have resolved on a deep link, so fall back to the\\r\\n  // narrow per-student RPC rather than showing a nameless header.\\r\\n  const { data: rpcEmail } = useStudentEmail(studentId ?? null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const isOnline = isUserOnline(student?.last_seen_at ?? null);\\r\\n  const lastActiveLabel = formatLastActiveLabel(student?.last_seen_at ?? null);\\r\\n  const lastActiveAbsolute = student?.last_seen_at\\r\\n    ? format(new Date(student.last_seen_at), \'MMM d, h:mm a\')\\r\\n    : null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const context = useMemo<DossierContext>(\\r\\n    () => ({\\r\\n      studentId: studentId as string,\\r\\n      student,\\r\\n      studentName,\\r\\n      studentEmail,\\r\\n      openAssign: () => setShowAssign(true),\\r\\n    }),\\r\\n    [student, studentEmail, studentId, studentName],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!studentId) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <section className=\\"flex h-full w-full flex-col\\" aria-label=\\"Student dossier\\">\\r\\n      <header className=\\"shrink-0 border-b border-black/10 px-5 pb-0 pt-4\\">\\r\\n        <button\\r\\n          type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n          onClick={() => navigate(\'/teacher/students\')}\\r\\n          className=\\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-black/45 transition hover:text-black\\"\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          <ArrowLeft className=\\"h-3.5 w-3.5\\" strokeWidth={1.5} />\\r\\n          Roster\\r\\n        </button>\\r\\n\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3\\">\\r\\n          <div className=\\"min-w-0\\">\\r\\n            <h1 className=\\"truncate text-[20px] font-semibold tracking-tight text-black\\">\\r\\n              {studentName || \'Student\'}\\r\\n            </h1>\\r\\n            <div className=\\"mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-black/45\\">\\r\\n              {studentEmail && <span className=\\"truncate font-mono\\">{studentEmail}</span>}\\r\\n              <span className=\\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5\\">\\r\\n                <Activity className=\\"h-3.5 w-3.5\\" strokeWidth={1.5} />\\r\\n                {isOnline ? \'Online on LSAT U\' : \'Offline\'}\\r\\n              </span>\\r\\n              <span>{lastActiveLabel}</span>\\r\\n              {lastActiveAbsolute && <span>· {lastActiveAbsolute}</span>}\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n          <button\\r\\n            type=\\"button\\"\\r\\n            onClick={() => setShowAssign(true)}\\r\\n            className=\\"inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 bg-teacher-accent px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-teacher-accent-hover\\"\\r\\n          >\\r\\n            <Plus className=\\"h-3.5 w-3.5\\" strokeWidth={2} />\\r\\n            Assign work\\r\\n          </button>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n        {/* Sub-tabs sit below the shell\'s room rail, so they take the ramp\'s\\r\\n            13px label step — subordinate to the rail\'s 14px without inventing\\r\\n            an undocumented size. */}\\r\\n        <nav className=\\"-mb-px mt-3 flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto\\" aria-label=\\"Dossier sections\\">\\r\\n          {TABS.map((tab) => (\\r\\n            <NavLink\\r\\n              key={tab.to}\\r\\n              to={tab.to}\\r\\n              className={({ isActive }) =>\\r\\n                cn(\\r\\n                  \'whitespace-nowrap border-b-2 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] transition\',\\r\\n                  isActive\\r\\n                    ? could not load.\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  if (rows.length === 0) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <section aria-label=\\"Signup queue\\" className=\\"border border-black/12 bg-white/25 px-4 py-3.5\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"mb-2.5 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"text-[10px] font-medium uppercase </div>\\r\\n      <ul className=\\"divide-y divide-black/10\\">\\r\\n        {rows.map((person) => {\\r\\n          const who = person.display_name || \'No name yet\';\\r\\n          const isPicking = number;\\r\\n  currency: string;\\r\\n  status: string;\\r\\n  paid_at: string;\\r\\n  student_id: string | null;\\r\\n  match_state: \'matched\' | \'unmatched\' | \'linked\' | \'ignored\';\\r\\n  offer_id: string | null;\\r\\n  grant_state: \'none\' | \'awaiting\' | \'granted\' | \'dismissed\';\\r\\n  granted_credits: number | null;\\r\\n  livemode: boolean;\\r\\n  source: \'webhook\' | money(cents: number, currency: string) {\\r\\n  return new Intl.NumberFormat(\'en-US\', { style: \'currency\', currency: currency.toUpperCase() || \'USD\' }).format(cents / 100);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Payments that need Joshua: a package waiting for a grant, or a new paid payer\\r\\n * nobody is linked to. Backfilled history was already handled by hand, so an\\r\\n * old unmatched charge stays in the list without asking for anything.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function needsAttention(p: StripePayment) {\\r\\n  if (p.grant_state === \'awaiting\') return true;\\r\\n  return p.match_state === \'unmatched\' && p.status === \'succeeded\' && p.source === \'webhook\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function matchesSearch(p: StripePayment, q: string, studentName: (id: string | null) => string | null) {\\r\\n  if (!q) return true;\\r\\n  const hay = [p.payer_name, p.email, p.description, p.product, studentName(p.student_id), money(p.amount_cents, p.currency)]\\r\\n    .filter(Boolean).join(\' \').toLowerCase();\\r\\n  return q.toLowerCase().split(/\\\\s+/).every((w) => hay.includes(w));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst STATUS_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {\\r\\n  succeeded: \'Paid\',\\r\\n  pending: \'Pending\',\\r\\n  failed: \'Failed\',\\r\\n  refunded: \'Refunded\',\\r\\n  partially_refunded: \'Part refunded\',\\r\\n  disputed: \'Disputed\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Every Stripe charge from the last three months, newest first, plus the two\\r\\n * things a payment can ask of Joshua: grant a package\'s passes, or say who paid.\\r\\n * Nothing here happens by itself (decisions/log.md, 2026-09-27).\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function PaymentsPanel() {\\r\\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\\r\\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(\'\');\\r\\n  const [shown, setShown] = useState(PAGE);\\r\\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [linking, setLinking] = useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [syncing, setSyncing] = useState(false);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const payments = useQuery<StripePayment[]>({\\r\\n    queryKey: PAYMENTS_KEY,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const since = new Date(Date.now() - 92 * 86400_000).toISOString();\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'stripe_payments\' as any)\\r\\n        .select(\'*\')\\r\\n        .gte(\'paid_at\', since)\\r\\n        .order(\'paid_at\', { ascending: false })\\r\\n        .limit(2000);\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      return (data ?? []) as unknown as StripePayment[];\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    staleTime: 30_000,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const lastSync = useQuery<{ finished_at: string | null; error: string | null } | null>({\\r\\n    queryKey: SYNC_KEY,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'stripe_sync_runs\' as any)\\r\\n        .select(\'finished_at,error\')\\r\\n        .order(\'started_at\', { ascending: false })\\r\\n        .limit(1)\\r\\n        .maybeSingle();\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      return data as any;\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    staleTime: 60_000,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const students = useQuery<ManagedUser[]>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'payments-students\'],\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => (await listManagedUsers()).filter((u) => u.role !== \'admin\'),\\r\\n    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const nameById = useMemo(() => {\\r\\n    const m = new Map<string, string>();\\r\\n    for (const s of students.data ?? []) m.set(s.class_id, s.display_name || s.email);\\r\\n    return m;\\r\\n  }, [students.data]);\\r\\n  const studentName = (id: string | null) => (id ? nameById.get(id) ?? null : null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  // A new or updated payment (webhook, sync, another tab) refreshes the list live.\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    const channel = supabase\\r\\n      .channel(`stripe-payments-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`)\\r\\n      .on(\'postgres_changes\', { event: \'*\', schema: \'public\', table: \'stripe_payments\' }, () => {\\r\\n        void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: PAYMENTS_KEY });\\r\\n      })\\r\\n      .subscribe();\\r\\n    return () => {\\r\\n      supabase.removeChannel(channel);\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }, [queryClient]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const rows = payments.data ?? [];\\r\\n  const attention = rows.filter(needsAttention);\\r\\n  const filtered = rows.filter((p) => matchesSearch(p, query.trim(), studentName));\\r\\n  const paidTotal = rows\\r\\n    .filter((p) => p.status === \'succeeded\' || p.status === \'partially_refunded\')\\r\\n    .reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.amount_cents - p.amount_refunded_cents, 0);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const refresh = () => {\\r\\n    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: PAYMENTS_KEY });\\r\\n    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: SYNC_KEY });\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const sync = async () => {\\r\\n    setSyncing(true);\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'stripe-sync\', { body: { days: 90 } });\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      toast.success(`Synced ${data?.charges ?? 0} Stripe charges`);\\r\\n      refresh();\\r\\n    } catch (error) {\\r\\n      console.error(\'stripe-sync failed\', error);\\r\\n      toast.error(\'Stripe sync failed\');\\r\\n    } finally {\\r\\n      setSyncing(false);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const rpc = async (p: StripePayment, fn: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, done: string) => {\\r\\n    setBusy(p.id);\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const { error } = await supabase.rpc(fn as any, { _payment_id: p.id, ...args } as any);\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      toast.success(done);\\r\\n      setLinking(null);\\r\\n      refresh();\\r\\n    } catch (error: any) {\\r\\n      console.error(`${fn} failed`, { id: p.id, error });\\r\\n      toast.error(error?.message ?? \'That did not work\');\\r\\n    } finally {\\r\\n      setBusy(null);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (payments.isError) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <div className=\\"border border-black/12 bg-white/25 px-4 py-3.5 text-[13px] text-black/50\\">\\r\\n        Payments could not load.\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const who = (p: StripePayment) => studentName(p.student_id) || p.payer_name || p.email || \'Unknown payer\';\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <section aria-label=\\"Payments\\" className=\\"border border-black/12 bg-white/25 px-4 py-3.5\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"mb-2.5 flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-3\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-black/40\\">\\r\\n"}'}]

Scan never auto-attaches.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <button\\r\\n          onClick={handleScan}\\r\\n          disabled={scan.isPending}\\r\\n          className=\\"inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:opacity-50\\"\\r\\n        >\\r\\n          {scan.isPending ? <Loader2 className=\\"h-4 w-4 animate-spin\\" /> : <RefreshCw className=\\"h-4 w-4\\" />}\\r\\n          Scan for new session notes\\r\\n        </button>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      {isLoading ? (\\r\\n        <p className=\\"mt-4 text-sm text-zinc-500\\">Loading queue…</p>\\r\\n      ) : queue.length === 0 ? (\\r\\n        <p className=\\"mt-4 text-sm text-zinc-500\\">Nothing waiting. Run a scan to detect new notes.</p>\\r\\n      ) : (\\r\\n        <ul className=\\"mt-4 space-y-3\\">\\r\\n          {queue.map((item) => {\\r\\n            const meta = (item.raw_meta ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;\\r\\n            return (\\r\\n              <li key={item.id} className=\\"rounded-md border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/40 p-3\\">\\r\\n                <div className=\\"flex items-start justify-between gap-3\\">\\r\\n                  <div className=\\"min-w-0\\">\\r\\n                    <p className=\\"truncate text-sm font-medium\\">{item.raw_subject ?? \\"(no subject)\\"}</p>\\r\\n                    <p className=\\"mt-0.5 text-xs text-zinc-500\\">\\r\\n                      {meta.parsed_name ? `Parsed name: ${String(meta.parsed_name)}` : \\"No name parsed\\"}\\r\\n                      {item.calendar_event_id ? \\" · calendar window hit\\" : \\"\\"}\\r\\n                      {` · confidence ${item.match_confidence ?? \\"low\\"}`}\\r\\n                    </p>\\r\\n                  </div>\\r\\n                </div>\\r\\n                <div className=\\"mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2\\">\\r\\n                  <select\\r\\n                    value={picks[item.id] ?? \\"\\"}\\r\\n                    onChange={(e) => setPicks((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: e.target.value }))}\\r\\n                    className=\\"rounded-md border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 px-2 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-zinc-500\\"\\r\\n                  >\\r\\n                    <option value=\\"\\">Select student…</option>\\r\\n                    {roster.map((s) => (\\r\\n                      <option key={s.class_id} value={s.class_id}>\\r\\n                        {s.display_name ?? \\"(no name)\\"}{s.email ? ` — ${s.email}` : \\"\\"}\\r\\n                      </option>\\r\\n                    ))}\\r\\n                  </select>\\r\\n                  <button\\r\\n                    onClick={() => handleResolve(item)}\\r\\n                    disabled={resolve.isPending}\\r\\n                    className=\\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-700 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-emerald-600 disabled:opacity-50\\"\\r\\n                  >\\r\\n                    <Check className=\\"h-4 w-4\\" /> Resolve\\r\\n                  </button>\\r\\n                  <button\\r\\n                    onClick={() => handleDiscard(item)}\\r\\n                    disabled={discard.isPending}\\r\\n                    className=\\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-zinc-700 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:opacity-50\\"\\r\\n                  >\\r\\n                    <X className=\\"h-4 w-4\\" /> Discard\\r\\n                  </button>\\r\\n                </div>\\r\\n              </li>\\r\\n            );\\r\\n          })}\\r\\n        </ul>\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n\\r\\n      {recent.length > 0 && (\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-6\\">\\r\\n          <h3 className=\\"text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500\\">Recent</h3>\\r\\n          <ul className=\\"mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs text-zinc-400\\">\\r\\n            {recent.map((item) => (\\r\\n              <li key={item.id} className=\\"flex justify-between gap-3\\">\\r\\n                <span className=\\"truncate\\">{item.raw_subject ?? item.gdrive_doc_id ?? item.id}</span>\\r\\n                <span className=\\"shrink-0 text-zinc-500\\">{item.status}</span>\\r\\n              </li>\\r\\n            ))}\\r\\n          </ul>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n    </section>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"q":1,"result":"      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      return jsonRes({ ok: true, status: \\"discarded\\", ingestion_id: ledgerId });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    // ── scan: detect + queue ──\\r\\n    if (action === \\"scan\\") {\\r\\n      // Caller may override the search; default anchors on the real Gemini Notes\\r\\n      // sender (gemini-notes@google.com) — the authoritative source signal — and\\r\\n      // requires a Google Doc link in a recent window. Anchoring on sender (not\\r\\n      // just the word \\"Notes\\" in any subject) is what makes this match the real\\r\\n      // Gmail pattern instead of over-matching unrelated mail.\\r\\n      const query: string = body.q ??\\r\\n        \'from:gemini-notes@google.com \\"docs.google.com/document\\" newer_than:14d\';\\r\\n      const max: number = Math.min(body.max ?? 25, 50);\\r\\n\\r\\n      const headers = gmailHeaders();\\r\\n      const listParams = new URLSearchParams({ maxResults: String(max), q: query });\\r\\n      const listRes = await fetch(`${GMAIL_GATEWAY}/users/me/messages?${listParams}`, { headers });\\r\\n      const listTxt = await listRes.text();\\r\\n      if (!listRes.ok) throw new Error(`Gmail list ${listRes.status}: ${listTxt.slice(0, 300)}`);\\r\\n      const messages: any[] = JSON.parse(listTxt || \\"{}\\").messages ?? [];\\r\\n\\r\\n      const detected: any[] = [];\\r\\n      let skippedExisting = 0;\\r\\n      let skippedNoDoc = 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n      for (const m of messages) {\\r\\n        const msgRes = await fetch(\\r\\n          `${GMAIL_GATEWAY}/users/me/messages/${m.id}?format=full`, { headers },\\r\\n        );\\r\\n        if (!msgRes.ok) continue;\\r\\n        const msg = JSON.parse(await msgRes.text());\\r\\n        const hdrs = msg.payload?.headers ?? [];\\r\\n        const subject = header(hdrs, \\"Subject\\");\\r\\n        const from = header(hdrs, \\"From\\");\\r\\n        const internalDate = msg.internalDate ? new Date(Number(msg.internalDate)).toISOString() : null;\\r\\n\\r\\n        const bodyText = decodeBody(msg.payload) + \\" \\" + (msg.snippet ?? \\"\\");\\r\\n        const docMatch = bodyText.match(DOC_ID_RE);\\r\\n        if (!docMatch) { skippedNoDoc++; continue; }\\r\\n        const docId = docMatch[1];\\r\\n        const nameMatch = subject.match(SUBJECT_NAME_RE);\\r\\n        const parsedName = nameMatch ? nameMatch[1].trim() : null;\\r\\n\\r\\n        // Dedup against the ledger by gmail id or doc id.\\r\\n        const { data: existing } = await admin\\r\\n          .from(\\"session_ingestions\\").select(\\"id\\")\\r\\n          .or(`gmail_message_id.eq.${m.id},gdrive_doc_id.eq.${docId}`)\\r\\n          .maybeSingle();\\r\\n        if (existing) { skippedExisting++; continue; }\\r\\n\\r\\n        // Calendar corroboration: time+title window only (schedule_events has\\r\\n        // no attendee emails — build-spec §3d). A hit raises confidence but\\r\\n        // never confirms identity on its own, so we keep status needs_match.\\r\\n        let calendarHit: any = null;\\r\\n        if (internalDate && parsedName) {\\r\\n          const lo = new Date(new Date(internalDate).getTime() - 6 * 3600_000).toISOString();\\r\\n          const hi = new Date(new Date(internalDate).getTime() + 6 * 3600_000).toISOString();\\r\\n          const { data: evs } = await admin\\r\\n            .from(\\"schedule_events\\").select(\\"id, title, start_at\\")\\r\\n            .gte(\\"start_at\\", lo).lte(\\"start_at\\", hi);\\r\\n          calendarHit = (evs ?? []).find((e: any) =>\\r\\n            (e.title ?? \\"\\").toLowerCase().includes(parsedName.toLowerCase()),\\r\\n          ) ?? null;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n\\r\\n        const rawMeta = {\\r\\n          gmail_message_id: m.id, gdrive_doc_id: docId, subject, from,\\r\\n          parsed_name: parsedName, internal_date: internalDate,\\r\\n          // The Gemini Notes email body carries a short summary + suggested next\\r\\n          // steps. Persist the snippet so the review queue shows context without\\r\\n          // opening the Doc (the Doc itself is read only on resolve).\\r\\n          email_summary: (msg.snippet ?? \\"\\").trim() || null,\\r\\n          calendar_window_hit: calendarHit ? { id: calendarHit.id, title: calendarHit.title } : null,\\r\\n        };\\r\\n\\r\\n        const { data: inserted, error: insErr } = await admin\\r\\n          .from(\\"session_ingestions\\")\\r\\n          .insert({\\r\\n            gmail_message_id: m.id,\\r\\n            gdrive_doc_id: docId,\\r\\n            calendar_event_id: calendarHit?.id ?? null,\\r\\n            status: \\"needs_match\\",\\r\\n            match_confidence: calendarHit ? \\"medium\\" : \\"low\\",\\r\\n            match_reason: parsedName\\r\\n              ? `parsed_name:${parsedName}${calendarHit ? \\"+calendar_window\\" : \\"\\"}`\\r\\n              : \\"no_name_parsed\\",\\r\\n            raw_subject: subject,\\r\\n            raw_meta: rawMeta,\\r\\n            attempt_count: 1,\\r\\n            last_attempt_at: new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n            scan_requested_by: caller.id,\\r\\n            created_by: caller.id,\\r\\n          })\\r\\n          .select(\\"id\\").single();\\r\\n        if (insErr) {\\r\\n          // Unique-constraint race — treat as already seen, don\'t fail the scan.\\r\\n          skippedExisting++;\\r\\n          continue;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        detected.push({ ingestion_id: inserted.id, doc_id: docId, parsed_name: parsedName, calendar_hit: !!calendarHit });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      return jsonRes({\\r\\n        ok: true,\\r\\n        scanned: messages.length,\\r\\n        queued: detected.length,\\r\\n        skipped_existing: skippedExisting,\\r\\n has_bootcamp_access: \\"Bootcamps\\",\\r\\n  has_journal_access: \\"Wrong answers\\",\\r\\n  has_flagged_access: \\"Flagged questions\\",\\r\\n  has_analytics_access: \\"Progress and history\\",\\r\\n \\"TA worksheets\\",\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nconst FLAG_KEYS = Object.keys(FLAG_LABELS) as (keyof HubStudentRow)[];\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Overview tab.
