studentId : null);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: attemptsCount, isLoading: attemptsLoading } = useQuery<number>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\\"hub-overview-attempts\\", studentId],\\r\\n    enabled: !!studentId,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const { count, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\\"attempts\\")\\r\\n        .select(\\"*\\", { count: \\"exact\\", head: true })\\r\\n        .eq(\\"class_id\\", studentId as string);\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      return count ?? 0;\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: wajCount, isLoading: wajLoading } = useQuery<number>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\\"hub-overview-waj\\", studentId],\\r\\n    enabled: !!studentId,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      // Mirror the ta-chat edge function: wrong_answer_journal is filtered\\r\\n      // by class_id on the admin side too.\\r\\n      const { count, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\\"wrong_answer_journal\\")\\r\\n        .select(\\"*\\", { count: \\"exact\\", head: true })\\r\\n        .eq(\\"class_id\\", studentId as string);\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      return count ?? This is that reader.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A row opens to the question-by-question record. Sections sat before that\\r\\n * record started being persisted say so, rather than opening onto an empty\\r\\n * table that reads as \\"you answered nothing\\".\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport React, { useState } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { ChevronDown } from \'lucide-react\';\\r\\nimport { cn } from \'@/lib/utils\';\\r\\nimport { formatDurationMs } from \'@/lib/drillHistory\';\\r\\nimport {\\r\\n  hasDetail,\\r\\n  sectionLabel,\\r\\n  summarizeSections,\\r\\n  type SectionHistoryRow,\\r\\n} from \'@/lib/sectionHistory\';\\r\\nimport { UNANSWERED } from \'@/lib/sectionHistorySave\';\\r\\nimport { CATEGORY_LABEL } from \'@/lib/sectionAnalysis\';\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction Tile({ label, value, sub }: { label: string; value: string; sub?: string }) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"rounded-xl border border-border bg-card p-4\\">\\r\\n      <p className=\\"text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground\\">{label}</p>\\r\\n      <p className=\\"mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold text-foreground\\">{value}</p>\\r\\n      {sub && <p className=\\"mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">{sub}</p>}\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** A filled proportion of the section, the shape 7Sage puts on every row. */\\r\\nfunction ScoreBar({ percent }: { percent: number }) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <span className=\\"flex h-1.5 w-16 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-muted\\" aria-hidden>\\r\\n      <span className=\\"h-full rounded-full bg-foreground/70\\" style={{ width: `${percent}%` }} />\\r\\n    </span>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction dateLabel(iso: string): string {\\r\\n  const d = new Date(iso);\\r\\n  const today = new Date();\\r\\n  const yesterday = new Date();\\r\\n  yesterday.setDate(today.getDate() - 1);\\r\\n  const same = (a: Date, b: Date) => a.toDateString() === b.toDateString();\\r\\n  if (same(d, today)) return \'Today\';\\r\\n  if (same(d, yesterday)) return \'Yesterday\';\\r\\n  return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: \'short\', day: \'numeric\', year: \'numeric\' });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction QuestionTable({ row }: { row: SectionHistoryRow }) {\\r\\n  if (!hasDetail(row)) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <p className=\\"px-4 pb-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n        The question-by-question record was not kept for this section. The score and\\r\\n        timing above are everything that was saved.\\r\\n      </p>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"overflow-x-auto px-4 pb-4\\">\\r\\n      <table className=\\"w-full min-w-[34rem] text-left text-xs\\">\\r\\n        <thead className=\\"text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n          <tr className=\\"border-b border-border\\">\\r\\n            <th className=\\"py-2 pr-3 font-medium\\">Q</th>\\r\\n            <th className=\\"py-2 pr-3 font-medium\\">Type</th>\\r\\n            <th className=\\"py-2 pr-3 font-medium\\">Level</th>\\r\\n            <th className=\\"py-2 pr-3 font-medium\\">Yours</th>\\r\\n            {row.brUsed && <th className=\\"py-2 pr-3 font-medium\\">BR</th>}\\r\\n            <th className=\\"py-2 pr-3 font-medium\\">Correct</th>\\r\\n            <th className=\\"py-2 font-medium\\">Time</th>\\r\\n          </tr>\\r\\n        </thead>\\r\\n        <tbody className=\\"divide-y divide-border\\">\\r\\n          {row.questions.map((q) => (\\r\\n            <tr key={q.qid}>\\r\\n              <td className=\\"py-2 pr-3 tabular-nums text-muted-foreground\\">{q.qnum}</td>\\r\\n              <td className=\\"py-2 pr-3 text-foreground\\">{q.qtype}</td>\\r\\n              <td className=\\"py-2 pr-3 tabular-nums text-muted-foreground\\">{q.difficulty}</td>\\r\\n              <td\\r\\n                className={cn(\\r\\n                  \'py-2 pr-3 font-medium tabular-nums\',\\r\\n                  q.initialAnswer === UNANSWERED\\r\\n                    ? \'text-muted-foreground\'\\r\\n                    : q.initialCorrect\\r\\n                      ? \'text-success\'\\r\\n                      : \'text-destructive\',\\r\\n                )}\\r\\n              >\\r\\n                {q.initialAnswer}\\r\\n              </td>\\r\\n              {row.brUsed && (\\r\\n                <td\\r\\n                  className={cn(\\r\\n                    \'py-2 pr-3 font-medium tabular-nums\',\\r\\n                    q.brAnswer === undefined\\r\\n                      ? \'text-muted-foreground\'\\r\\n                      : q.brCorrect\\r\\n                        ? \'text-success\'\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

The teacher dashboard already has signup, payment, message, task, and calendar feeds. The main gaps are a fuller upcoming session view, student analytics with question-level homework review, and a post-session transcript task. Gemini Notes ingestion and recap review exist, but matching and scanning currently require teacher action. These findings come from code inspection; I did not verify them in the live app this turn.

## assistant

I’ll check how signup decisions and access tiers are represented in the code, including what “pending grant” actually means.

NULL = undecided (signup queue). Label only: the has_*_access switches are what the app enforces.\';\\r\\n\\r\\n-- The tier is an admin act, like the switches it names.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_classroom_access OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_analytics_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_analytics_access OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_schedule_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_schedule_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_waj_access       OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_journal_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_journal_access   OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_flagged_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_flagged_access   OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_chat_access      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_chat_access      OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_export_access    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_export_access    OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_ta_access        IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_ta_access        OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_activity_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_activity_access  OR\\r\\n     NEW.access_tier          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.access_tier          OR\\r\\n     NEW.archived_at          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at          OR\\r\\n     NEW.archived_reason      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_reason      OR\\r\\n     NEW.proficiency_level    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.proficiency_level    OR\\r\\n     NEW.class_id             IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.class_id             THEN\\r\\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'profiles: access flags, access tier, archive state, proficiency_level and class_id are admin-controlled\';\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  RETURN NEW;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$function$;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Drift: a Tier 1 or Tier 3 label only holds while the and left alone, which is what lets set_tier move a row between templates.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.profiles_access_tier_drift()\\r\\n RETURNS trigger\\r\\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\\r\\n SET RETURN NEW;\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n  IF TG_OP = \'UPDATE\' AND NEW.access_tier IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.access_tier THEN\\r\\n    RETURN NEW;\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  all_on :=\\r\\n    NEW.has_drill_access AND NEW.has_practice_access AND NEW.has_activity_access AND\\r\\n    NEW.has_bootcamp_access AND NEW.has_journal_access AND NEW.has_flagged_access AND\\r\\n    NEW.has_analytics_access AND NEW.has_export_access AND NEW.has_classroom_access AND\\r\\n    NEW.has_chat_access AND NEW.has_schedule_access AND NEW.has_ta_access;\\r\\n\\r\\n  floor_only :=\\r\\n    NEW.has_classroom_access AND NEW.has_chat_access AND NEW.has_schedule_access AND NOT (\\r\\n      NEW.has_drill_access OR NEW.has_practice_access OR NEW.has_activity_access OR\\r\\n      NEW.has_bootcamp_access OR NEW.has_journal_access OR NEW.has_flagged_access OR\\r\\n      NEW.has_analytics_access OR NEW.has_export_access OR NEW.has_ta_access\\r\\n    );\\r\\n\\r\\n  IF NEW.access_tier = \'tier_1\' AND NOT all_on THEN\\r\\n    NEW.access_tier := \'tier_2\';\\r\\n  ELSIF NEW.access_tier = \'tier_3\' AND NOT floor_only THEN\\r\\n    NEW.access_tier := \'tier_2\';\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  RETURN NEW;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$function$;\\r\\n\\r\\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS profiles_access_tier_drift_trg ON public.profiles;\\r\\nCREATE TRIGGER '{"q":4,"result":"-- Access tiers, stage 3: give every existing account a tier, and start new\\r\\n-- signups at the floor.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- Runs after stage 1, so the twelve switches already mean what they say.\\r\\n-- Joshua\'s instruction (2026-09-24): nobody\'s current access changes, with one\\r\\n-- exception, Sandra, who goes to Tier 1. So existing accounts are labelled by\\r\\n-- the switches they already have, and no switch moves except Sandra\'s.\\r\\n-- Admins are all-on and land on Tier 1; the label does nothing for an admin.\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Sandra: Tier 1 by Joshua\'s decision.\\r\\nUPDATE public.profiles SET\\r\\n  access_tier          = \'tier_1\',\\r\\n  has_drill_access     = true,\\r\\n  has_practice_access  = true,\\r\\n  has_activity_access  = true,\\r\\n  has_bootcamp_access  = true,\\r\\n  has_journal_access   = true,\\r\\n  has_flagged_access   = true,\\r\\n  has_analytics_access = true,\\r\\n  has_export_access    = true,\\r\\n  has_classroom_access = true,\\r\\n  has_chat_access      = true,\\r\\n  has_schedule_access  = true,\\r\\n  has_ta_access        = true\\r\\nWHERE class_id = \'b0d1ec29-5946-4a1b-a649-60038683df32\';\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Everyone with all twelve on.\\r\\nUPDATE public.profiles SET access_tier = \'tier_1\'\\r\\nWHERE access_tier IS NULL AND archived_at IS NULL\\r\\n  AND has_drill_access AND has_practice_access AND has_activity_access AND\\r\\n      has_bootcamp_access AND has_journal_access AND has_flagged_access AND\\r\\n      has_analytics_access AND has_export_access AND has_classroom_access AND\\r\\n      has_chat_access AND has_schedule_access AND has_ta_access;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Everyone else keeps exactly the switches they have, labelled Tier 2. This\\r\\n-- includes the classroom-only group: Tier 3 would add Messages and Schedule,\\r\\n-- which is a change Joshua has not made. He can move them to Tier 3 himself.\\r\\nUPDATE public.profiles SET access_tier = \'tier_2\'\\r\\nWHERE access_tier IS NULL AND archived_at IS NULL;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- New signups: the floor, undecided. has_chat_access and has_schedule_access\\r\\n-- already default to true and every other switch to false; Classroom joins the\\r\\n-- floor so a new student has somewhere to stand while they wait in the queue.\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\\r\\n  ALTER COLUMN has_classroom_access SET DEFAULT true,\\r\\n  ALTER COLUMN access_tier          SET DEFAULT NULL;\\r\\n"}'}]

toast.error(\'The primary admin account cannot be demoted.\');\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      setBusy((prev) => ({ ...prev, role: true }));\\r\\n      try {\\r\\n        const res = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', {\\r\\n          method: \'POST\',\\r\\n          body: { action: \'set_role\', user_id: studentId, role },\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        if pending) return pending[field];\\r\\n      return Boolean(source?.[field]);\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    [pending],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  return { toggleAccess, setRole, readFlag, isBusy: (field: string) => ## assistant

The signup queue means **a non-admin, non-archived account with no access tier selected**. It is separate from a payment awaiting a package pass grant. Access tiers set feature switches: Tier 1 enables all current features, Tier 2 keeps a custom selection, and Tier 3 provides Classroom, Messages, Schedule, and assigned work. These are code findings; I made no changes.

## assistant

## assistant

Teacher Today currently reads only about 24 hours of events, even though the Google Calendar sync stores events up to 90 days ahead. Expanding that view is small. Calendar attachments, including the Gemini Notes documents, are currently discarded during sync, so linking those notes and tracking post-session follow-up needs additional stored fields and a durable completion state. I did not change code or verify the production cron.

\\"\\\\n\\\\n--- LR Strategies: Sufficient Assumption ---\\\\n**Question Stem Keywords**\\\\nif assumed, conclusion follows logically, conclusion is properly drawn, conclusion is properly inferred\\\\n\\\\n**Examples**\\\\nWhich of the following, if assumed, would allow the conclusion to follow logically?\\\\n\\\\nThe conclusion above is properly drawn if which of the following is assumed?\\\\n\\\\n**Reading Goal**\\\\nFind the Conclusion. Find the Support.\\\\n\\\\nAsk yourself if there\'s a New Term or idea in the Conclusion. (If so, that idea needs to be in the correct answer).\\\\n\\\\nAsk yourself what the Evidence told us about each half of the conclusion. (if the conclusion were \\\\\\"Eddie is a great friend\\\\\\", look to see what facts were provided about Eddie, and look to see if they provided a rule that says \\\\\\"If X, then Great Friend\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\nIf there is conditional logic, mentally or physically diagram it. Link conditionals together where possible.\\\\n\\\\nThink about the missing conditional piece you would need to get from the Subject of the conclusion (Eddie) to the Predicate (is a great friend).\\\\n\\\\nIf there isn\'t conditional logic, try to think of the one way that Conclusion *wouldn\'t* be true. The correct answer would need to rule out that possibility.\\\\n\\\\n**Prephrase Goal**\\\\nSolve for the correct answer ahead of time!\\\\n\\\\nMost of the time we\'re pre-phrasing a conditional idea, trying to get *from this idea, to that idea*.\\\\nThe correct answer loves to put that connection in contrapositive form, so if we\'re thinking,\\\\n\\\\\\"I need to hear that *golfing requires money*\\\\\\"\\\\nthen we remind ourselves that it might sound like\\\\n\\\\\\"if you don\'t have money, you can\'t golf\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n**Correct Answer**\\\\nWhich answer, when added to the evidence, guarantees the truth of the conclusion?\\\\n\\\\nCorrect answer will do one of these things:\\\\n- provide the missing link that completes a chain of ideas\\\\n- provide the missing fact that triggers a conditional\\\\n- rule out the one possible way the conclusion could be wrong\\\\n- create a situation where whether X is true or X is false, the conclusion is true.\\\\n\\\\n**Wrong Answers**\\\\n**Right Ideas, Wrong Order** -illegal negations or reversals of the link we were seeking\\\\n\\\\n**Lacks Crucial Term** - most commonly this means that the answer lacks the undefined New Idea in the conclusion, so it\'s powerless to prove that New Idea.\\\\n\\\\n**Too Weak** - we have to fully prove the conclusion, so \\\\\\"some\\\\\\" or \\\\\\"most\\\\\\" ain\'t usually gonna cut it.\\\\n\\\\n--- LSAT Lab Breakdown: Sufficient Assumption ---\\\\n<table>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <th>STIMULUS</th>\\\\n        <th>READING STIMULUS</th>\\\\n        <th>JUDGING ANSWERS</th>\\\\n        <th>WRONG ANSWERS</th>\\\\n    </tr>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <td>arguments, reasoning</td>\\\\n        <td>**Find the Conclusion**<br>Separate Conc. into Naming &amp; Telling part.<br>Ex: Thus, gray cats are creepy roommates.<br>Did arg provide rule for Telling Part?<br>If the Evidence didn\'t provide a rule like<br>[__] -&gt; Creepy Roommate<br>then the right answer must be such a rule.<br><br>If Evid. did provide a rule, we need to know that the Naming Part (gray cats) triggers it. Otherwise, argue Anti-conc. to find a gap.</td>\\\\n        <td>- If there was a New Idea in the Conclusion, does this answer address the New Idea?<br>- If conditional, is this a reversal or negation of what I wanted (wrong) or the contrapositive of what I wanted (correct)?<br>- If I combine this answer with facts from evidence, can I logically derive the conclusion?<br>- Does this go from what we do know to what we need to know?</td>\\\\n        <td>**Too Weak** less than certain = red flag<br>**Out of Scope** unmentioned ideas are almost always wrong.<br>**Reversed/Negated Logic** appealing because it has the right ideas but in the wrong order.<br>**Inference / Nec Assumption** right answer for a different question type, but doesn\'t prove the conclusion.<br><br>**RIGHT ANSWERS**<br>95% link language within the arg.<br>5% rule out a potential Objection</td>\\\\n    </tr>\\\\n</table>\\\\n**PREDICT ANSWER?** High\\\\n**STEM KEYWORDS** if assumed, conclusion follows logically\\\\n\\\\n<table>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <th>Method</th>\\\\n        <th>rank 9th</th>\\\\n        <th>READING STIMULUS</th>\\\\n        <th>JUDGING ANSWERS</th>\\\\n        <th>WRONG ANSWERS</th>\\\\n    </tr>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <td>STIMULUS arguments, often two speakers</td>\\\\n        <td>These almost never involve conditional logic. More about plans / judgments / causal explanations.</td>\\\\n        <td>One-Speaker Stimulus Find Co…1537 tokens truncated…S</td>\\\\n        <td>WRONG ANSWERS</td>\\\\n    </tr>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <td>STIMULUS arguments</td>\\\\n        <td>Lots of Nec vs. Suff (illegal reversals or negations), bad quantifier math, Part vs. Whole, ad hom, causal, and unproven vs. proven false</td>\\\\n        <td>Conditional Logic? If we see any conditional premise, it\'s almost guaranteed that the argument will perform an illegal reversal / negation. Other Famous Flaw? Part vs. Whole is 2nd most common. if it\'s a rebuttal, look out for Ad Hom and Unproven v. Untrue.</td>\\\\n        <td>Defer on the Worst Answers Have a \\\\\\"must-have\\\\\\" or \\\\\\"core ingredient\\\\\\" you want to find in each answer, and if it\'s not there move on. e.g. Are you checking for similar conc? Are you checking for a conditional premise? An overlapping term? 1st pass: be picky about the arg having the same type / strength / number of claims. 2nd pass: think about \\\\\\"essence\\\\\\" and tolerate imperfect matches.</td>\\\\n        <td>Bad Evidence Match you don\'t get as many prem\'s as you need, they aren\'t the right style/strength or don\'t overlap in the correct way. Bad Conclusion Match the conc is not the same type or strength of claim as the original argument\'s. Bad Validity Match orig arg\'s logic was flawed the answer\'s is valid.</td>\\\\n    </tr>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <td>PREDICT ANSWER? medium</td>\\\\n        <td>We summarize the orig argument in the form of an Abstract Recipe, a famous flaw, or generic objection.</td>\\\\n        <td>Otherwise ... if there are quantifiers or copycat ideas, maybe diagram it. If not, just argue the anti-conclusion but make sure your objection isn\'t topic specific. Keep it generic like, \\\\\\"couldn\'t both things be false?\\\\\\"</td>\\\\n        <td></td>\\\\n        <td>RIGHT ANSWERS The best available match. More important to match flaw than to match structure perfectly.</td>\\\\n    </tr>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <td>STEM KEYWORDS most similar, flawed / questionable</td>\\\\n        <td>The pattern of flawed reasoning in the argument is most similar to which of the following?</td>\\\\n        <td></td>\\\\n        <td></td>\\\\n        <td></td>\\\\n    </tr>\\\\n</table>\\\\n\\\\n<table>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <th>Agree/Disagree</th>\\\\n        <th>rank 13th</th>\\\\n        <th>READING STIMULUS</th>\\\\n        <th>JUDGING ANSWERS</th>\\\\n        <th>WRONG ANSWERS</th>\\\\n    </tr>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <td>STIMULUS two speakers</td>\\\\n        <td>1st person always makes argument. 2nd person often does. But more important to read as statements.</td>\\\\n        <td>Read Both + Be a Good Mediator Listen carefully and try to understand on what level the 2nd person is disagreeing.</td>\\\\n        <td>- can I support that one of these speakers would Agree with this? (if not, eliminate)</td>\\\\n        <td>Too Strong can\'t support that anyone has endorsed a claim this extreme.</td>\\\\n    </tr>\\\\n    <tr>\\\\n        <td>PREDICT ANSWER? medium-high</td>\\\\n        <td>We\'ll either intuitively hear the disconnect, or we\'ll go claim by claim through P1\'s statements to figure out which claim (or assump) P2 dislikes.</td>\\\\n        <td>If you aren\'t sure, then revisit P1\'s statements one by one. 'input_text', 'text': '{"q":1,"result":"  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Teacher links an unmatched (or wrongly matched) payment to a student.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_link_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid, _student_id uuid)\\r\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\r\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\r\\nDECLARE _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\r\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\\r\\n    SET student_id = _student_id,\\r\\n        match_state = CASE WHEN _student_id IS NULL THEN \'ignored\' ELSE \'linked\' END,\\r\\n        grant_state = CASE\\r\\n          WHEN grant_state = \'none\' AND offer_id IS NOT NULL AND _student_id IS NOT NULL AND status = \'succeeded\' THEN \'awaiting\'\\r\\n          ELSE grant_state END,\\r\\n        updated_at = now()\\r\\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\\r\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\r\\n  RETURN _row;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Teacher confirms a package payment: add its credits to the student\'s pass.\\r\\n-- Idempotent: a payment already granted returns unchanged.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)\\r\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\r\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\r\\nDECLARE\\r\\n  _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\r\\n  _offer public.booking_offers%ROWTYPE;\\r\\n  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\r\\n  SELECT * INTO _row FROM public.stripe_payments WHERE id = _payment_id FOR UPDATE;\\r\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\r\\n  IF _row.grant_state = \'granted\' THEN RETURN _row; END IF;\\r\\n  IF _row.student_id IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'link_a_student_first\'; END IF;\\r\\n  IF _row.status <> \'succeeded\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'payment_not_settled\'; END IF;\\r\\n  SELECT * INTO _offer FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _row.offer_id AND provider = \'stripe\';\\r\\n  IF NOT FOUND OR _offer.credits <= 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'not_a_package_offer\'; END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = _row.student_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE;\\r\\n  IF FOUND THEN\\r\\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes\\r\\n      SET total_credits = total_credits + _offer.credits, status = \'active\', expires_at = NULL\\r\\n      WHERE id = _pass.id;\\r\\n  ELSE\\r\\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_passes (student_id, student_email, student_name, total_credits, used_credits, session_length_minutes, status)\\r\\n      VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.email, COALESCE(_row.payer_name, _row.email, \'Student\'), _offer.credits, 0, 70, \'active\');\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (student_id, offer_id, provider, provider_event_id, event_type, email, amount_cents, currency)\\r\\n    VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.offer_id, \'stripe\', \'charge:\' || _row.stripe_charge_id, \'paid\', _row.email, _row.amount_cents, _row.currency)\\r\\n    ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\\r\\n\\r\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\\r\\n    SET grant_state = \'granted\', granted_credits = _offer.credits, granted_at = now(), granted_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()\\r\\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\\r\\n  RETURN _row;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)\\r\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\r\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\r\\nDECLARE _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\r\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\\r\\n    SET grant_state = CASE WHEN grant_state = \'awaiting\' THEN \'dismissed\' ELSE grant_state END,\\r\\n        match_state = CASE WHEN match_state = \'unmatched\' THEN \'ignored\' ELSE match_state END,\\r\\n        updated_at = now()\\r\\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\\r\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\r\\n  RETURN _row;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n\\r\\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_link_stripe_payment(uuid, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\\r\\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\\r\\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\\r\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_link_stripe_payment(uuid, uuid) TO authenticated;\\r\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) TO authenticated;\\r\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION (\\r\\n        <p className=\\"text-[13px] text-black/45\\">No payment matches that search.</p>\\r\\n      ) : (\\r\\n        <>\\r\\n          <ul className=\\"divide-y divide-black/10\\">\\r\\n            {filtered.slice(0, shown).map((p) => (\\r\\n              <li key={p.id} className=\\"flex items-baseline gap-3 py-1.5 text-[13px]\\">\\r\\n                <span className=\\"w-[76px] shrink-0 tabular-nums text-black/50\\">\\r\\n                  {new Date(p.paid_at).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: \'short\', day: \'numeric\' })}\\r\\n                </span>\\r\\n                <span className=\\"min-w-0 flex-1 truncate\\">\\r\\n                  {who(p)}\\r\\n                  {p.product && <span className=\\"text-black/40\\"> &middot; {p.product.replace(\'_\', \' \')}</span>}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"q":3,"result":"  try {\\r\\n    event = await stripe.webhooks.constructEventAsync(\\r\\n      raw,\\r\\n      signature,\\r\\n      requiredEnv(\\"STRIPE_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET\\"),\\r\\n      300,\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  } catch (_) {\\r\\n    return json({ error: \\"invalid_signature\\" }, 400);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Every charge lands in the teacher\'s payments ledger. The ledger is a view,\\r\\n  // never a trigger, so a failure here is logged and never makes Stripe retry.\\r\\n  const ledgerChargeId = await chargeIdForLedger(event, stripeSecret).catch(() => null);\\r\\n  if (ledgerChargeId) {\\r\\n    try {\\r\\n      const { inserted, row } = await recordCharge(supabase, stripeSecret, ledgerChargeId, \\"webhook\\");\\r\\n      // Every new paid charge tells the admins once, whatever it paid for:\\r\\n      // payment links, the native single session, and Cal.com bookings alike.\\r\\n      // Only the writer that created the ledger row announces it.\\r\\n      if (inserted && row.status === \\"succeeded\\") {\\r\\n        await notifyAdminsOfPayment(supabase, {\\r\\n          email: row.email,\\r\\n          name: row.payer_name,\\r\\n          amountCents: row.amount_cents,\\r\\n          currency: row.currency,\\r\\n          awaitingGrant: row.grant_state === \\"awaiting\\",\\r\\n          unmatched: !row.student_id,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        await emailJoshuaOfPayment(supabase, row);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    } catch (err) {\\r\\n      console.error(\\"ledger write failed\\", { event: event.id, type: event.type, error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (event.type.startsWith(\\"charge.\\")) return json({ received: true });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (event.type.startsWith(\\"refund.\\")) {\\r\\n    const refund = event.data.object as Stripe.Refund;\\r\\n    if (event.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: \\"livemode_mismatch\\" }, 400);\\r\\n    const bookingId = String(refund.metadata?.booking_id || \\"\\");\\r\\n    if (!bookingId || !refund.id || !refund.status) return json({ error: \\"invalid_refund\\" }, 400);\\r\\n    const { error } = await supabase.rpc(\\"record_single_session_refund_status\\", {\\r\\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\\r\\n      _stripe_refund_id: refund.id,\\r\\n      _provider_status: refund.status,\\r\\n    });\\r\\n    if (error) throw error;\\r\\n    return json({ received: true });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (event.type === \\"checkout.session.expired\\") {\\r\\n    const session = event.data.object;\\r\\n    if (session.metadata?.product !== \\"single_session\\") return json({ received: true, ignored: \\"not_single_session\\" });\\r\\n    if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: \\"livemode_mismatch\\" }, 400);\\r\\n    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || \\"\\");\\r\\n    if (!bookingId || !session.id) return json({ error: \\"invalid_session\\" }, 400);\\r\\n    const { error } = await supabase.rpc(\\"expire_single_session_checkout\\", {\\r\\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\\r\\n      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,\\r\\n"}'}]

KEY,\\r\\n  label text NOT NULL,\\r\\n  provider text NOT NULL CHECK (provider IN (\'stripe\', \'calcom\')),\\r\\n  provider_ref text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\\r\\n  url text NOT NULL,\\r\\n  credits integer label, provider, provider_ref, url, credits)\\r\\nVALUES\\r\\n  (\'single-session\', \'Single session\', \'calcom\', \'session-link\', \'https://cal.com/joshua-jtx5ud/session-link\', 0),\\r\\n  (\'package-5\', \'5-hour package\', \'stripe\', \'plink_1STvqYIULVCj6rqqJb70Prhb\', \'https://buy.stripe.com/4gMaEY05F3dc9x13NqdZ61x\', 5),\\r\\n  (\'package-10\', \'10-hour package\', \'stripe\', provider_ref = excluded.provider_ref, url = excluded.url, credits = excluded.credits;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.student_booking_offers (\\r\\n  student_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  PRIMARY KEY (student_id, offer_id)\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS students.\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.booking_offers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS student_visible boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true;\\r\\n\\r\\nINSERT INTO public.booking_offers (id, label, provider, s\\r\\n    JOIN public.booking_offers o ON o.id = s.offer_id\\r\\n    WHERE s.student_id = _student_id AND s.enabled\\r\\n      AND (o.active OR (o.id = \'single-session\'\\r\\n        AND NULLIF(trim(s.url_override), \'\') IS NOT NULL\\r\\n        AND trim(s.url_override) NOT LIKE \'/%\'))\\r\\n  )\\r\\n  SELECT * FROM default_offers\\r\\n  UNION ALL\\r\\n  SELECT * FROM arranged_offers;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Label historical ledger rows with their package (history never asks for a grant).\\r\\nUPDATE public.stripe_payments p\\r\\n  SET offer_id = o.id, product = COALESCE(p.product, \'package\'), updated_at = now()\\r\\n  FROM public.booking_offers o\\r\\n  WHERE o.provider = \'stripe\' AND o.provider_ref = p.payment_link_id AND p.offer_id IS NULL;\\r\\n"}'}, ## assistant

The teacher can set a single-session price and use existing package links, but cannot currently create a student-specific Stripe link for a custom package. The old custom pass creator grants booking credits without payment and its route now redirects.

A safe dashboard generator would create a one-time Checkout Session tied to a stored student and package quote. After Stripe confirms payment, it would appear in Payments for teacher review and a one-click pass grant. This requires new code; I made no changes.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"q":0,"result":"// Admin-only booking pass management: create / list / revoke / rotate.\\r\\n// Every issued link uses the strong /book/:slug public route.\\r\\nimport { createClient } from \\"npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2\\";\\r\\nimport { corsHeaders } from \\"npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2/cors\\";\\r\\nimport { generateBookingSlug, validateNewPassInput } from \\"../_shared/bookingPassLinks.ts\\";\\r\\nimport { deleteEvent } from \\"../_shared/googleCalendar.ts\\";\\r\\nimport { findAuthUserIdByEmail } from \\"../_shared/linkPassToAccount.ts\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function sha256Hex(input: string): Promise<string> {\\r\\n  const buf = await crypto.subtle.digest(\\"SHA-256\\", new TextEncoder().encode(input));\\r\\n  return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \\"0\\")).join(\\"\\");\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction genToken(): string {\\r\\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(32);\\r\\n  crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);\\r\\n  return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...bytes))\\r\\n    .replace(/\\\\+/g, \\"-\\").replace(/\\\\//g, \\"_\\").replace(/=+$/g, \\"\\");\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst APP_ORIGIN = Deno.env.get(\\"APP_ORIGIN\\") || \\"https://lsatprep.study\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction bookingUrl(slug: string): string {\\r\\n  return `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/${slug}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction json(body: unknown, status = 200) {\\r\\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\\r\\n    status,\\r\\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, \\"Content-Type\\": \\"application/json\\" },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\\r\\n  if (req.method === \\"OPTIONS\\") return new Response(\\"ok\\", { headers: corsHeaders });\\r\\n  try {\\r\\n    const authHeader = req.headers.get(\\"Authorization\\") ?? \\"\\";\\r\\n    if (!authHeader.startsWith(\\"Bearer \\")) return json({ error: \\"unauthorized\\" }, 401);\\r\\n    const token = authHeader.slice(7);\\r\\n\\r\\n    const supabase = createClient(\\r\\n      Deno.env.get(\\"SUPABASE_URL\\")!,\\r\\n      Deno.env.get(\\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\\")!,\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    // Validate the caller JWT with the anon client: getClaims() supports the\\r\\n    // asymmetric signing-keys system, unlike getUser() on a service-role client.\\r\\n    const authClient = createClient(\\r\\n      Deno.env.get(\\"SUPABASE_URL\\")!,\\r\\n\\r\\n    if (action === \\"create\\") {\\r\\n      const validated = validateNewPassInput({\\r\\n        totalCredits: body.total_credits,\\r\\n        sessionLengthMinutes: body.session_length_minutes,\\r\\n        expiresAt: body.expires_at,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      if (!validated.ok) return json({ error: validated.error }, 400);\\r\\n      const { totalCredits, sessionLengthMinutes: sessionLen, expiresAt } = validated.value;\\r\\n      const raw = genToken();\\r\\n      const hash = await sha256Hex(raw);\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Passes created by name/email only are invisible to the signed-in\\r\\n      // surfaces (in-app booking page, uploads, schedule history). If the email\\r\\n      // matches a real account, attach it now so both doors agree.\\r\\n      if (!body.student_id && body.student_email) {\\r\\n        body.student_id = await findAuthUserIdByEmail(supabase, body.student_email);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // When granting to a real student account, go through grant_booking_access\\r\\n      // so any prior active pass is atomically revoked (one-active-per-student)\\r\\n      // AND the public booking slug is created in the same transaction.\\r\\n      if (body.student_id) {\\r\\n        // Generate slug + retry on the (rare) unique collision.\\r\\n        let lastErr: unknown = null;\\r\\n        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {\\r\\n          const slug = generateBookingSlug(body.student_name, body.student_email);\\r\\n          const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc(\\"grant_booking_access\\", {\\r\\n            _student_id: body.student_id,\\r\\n            _student_name: body.student_name || null,\\r\\n            _student_email: body.student_email || null,\\r\\n            _total_credits: totalCredits,\\r\\n            _session_length_minutes: sessionLen,\\r\\n            _expires_at: expiresAt,\\r\\n            _created_by: user.id,\\r\\n            _token_hash: hash,\\r\\n            _booking_slug: slug,\\r\\n          });\\r\\n          if (!error) return json({ pass: data, booking_url: bookingUrl(slug) });\\r\\n          const msg = String(error.message || \\"\\");\\r\\n          if (msg.includes(\\"student_id_required\\")) return json({ error: \\"student_id_required\\" }, 400);\\r\\n          if ((error as { code?: string }).code === \\"23505\\") { lastErr = error; continue; } // slug collision → retry\\r\\n          throw error;\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        console.error(\\"booking slug collision\\", lastErr);\\r\\n        return json({ error: \\"slug_collision\\" }, 409);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Manual passes use the same strong public-link path as account-backed passes.\\r\\n      let lastErr: unknown = null;\\r\\n      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt++) {\\r\\n        const slug = generateBookingSlug(body.student_name, body.student_email);\\r\\n        const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc(\\"create_manual_booking_pass\\", {\\r\\n          _student_name: body.student_name || null,\\r\\n          _student_email: body.student_email || null,\\r\\n          _total_credits: totalCredits,\\r\\n          _session_length_minutes: sessionLen,\\r\\n          _expires_at: expiresAt,\\r\\n          _notes: body.notes || null,\\r\\n          _created_by: user.id,\\r\\n          _token_hash: hash,\\r\\n          _booking_slug: slug,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        if (!error) return json({ pass: data, booking_url: bookingUrl(slug) });\\r\\n        if ((error as { code?: string }).code === \\"23505\\") { lastErr = error; continue; }\\r\\n        throw error;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      console.error(\\"booking slug collision\\", lastErr);\\r\\n      return json({ error: \\"slug_collision\\" }, 409);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n\\r\\n    if (action === \\"get_availability\\") {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc(\\"get_booking_availability\\");\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      return json(data ?? { timezone: \\"America/Toronto\\", ranges: [] });\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"q":1,"result":"import { useMemo, useState } from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { useQueryClient } from \\"@tanstack/react-query\\";\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \\"@/integrations/supabase/client\\";\\r\\nimport { edgeErrorText } from \\"@/lib/edgeFunctionError\\";\\r\\nimport CalendlyBookingCard, { type BookingApi } from \\"@/components/booking/CalendlyBookingCard\\";\\r\\nimport BookingOfferHub, { type BookingCommerceItem, type BookingOffer, type ExternalBookingItem } from \\"@/components/booking/BookingOfferHub\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n// The student-facing booking surface, with no page chrome of its own so it can\\r\\n// sit inside the Schedule shell as a tab.\\r\\n//\\r\\n// Order is deliberate and is the fix for the audit\'s worst finding: a student\\r\\n// holding paid credits used to land on a three-up sales panel, with the two\\r\\n// loudest buttons on the page both saying \\"Buy\\", and their actual calendar more\\r\\n// than a screen further down. State and calendar lead; the offers stay\\r\\n// reachable but demoted (2026-08-13 decision: demote, do not hide).\\r\\n\\r\\nconst invoke = async (action: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => {\\r\\n  // Wait for the session so we never send the anon key as the Bearer [REDACTED] —\\r\\n  // the function reads that as a token with no user and answers 401.\\r\\n  const { waitForAccessToken } = await import(\\"@/lib/waitForSession\\");\\r\\n  const token = await waitForAccessToken();\\r\\n  if (!token) throw new Error(\\"Please sign in again to load your booking.\\");\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke(\\"student-booking\\", { body: { action, ...extra } });\\r\\n  // supabase-js discards the response body on a non-2xx, leaving only the\\r\\n  // generic wrapper string. Read the real `{ error }` back off the Response so\\r\\n  // the code (slot_conflict, no_active_pass, ...) survives to the UI, which\\r\\n  // maps it to something the student can act on.\\r\\n  if (error) throw new Error(await edgeErrorText(error));\\r\\n  if ((data as { error?: string })?.error) throw new Error((data as { error: string }).error);\\r\\n  return data;\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function BookingView() {\\r\\n  const queryClient = useQueryClient();\\r\\n  const [commerce, setCommerce] = useState<{ offers: BookingOffer[]; commerce: BookingCommerceItem[]; external_bookings: ExternalBookingItem[]; bookings: { source: \\"redemption\\" | \\"single_session\\"; id: string; start_at: string; status: string }[] } | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [hasCredits, setHasCredits] = useState(false);\\r\\n\\r\\n  const api: BookingApi = useMemo(() => ({\\r\\n    loadInfo: async () => {\\r\\n      const d = (await invoke(\\"info\\")) as {\\r\\n        pass: { credits_remaining: number; session_length_minutes: number; total_credits: number; status: string } | null;\\r\\n        bookings?: { source: \\"redemption\\" | \\"single_session\\"; id: string; start_at: string; status: string; meet_url?: string | null }[];\\r\\n        offers?: BookingOffer[];\\r\\n        commerce?: BookingCommerceItem[];\\r\\n        external_bookings?: ExternalBookingItem[];\\r\\n      };\\r\\n      setCommerce({ offers: d.offers ?? [] });\\r\\n      const p = d.pass;\\r\\n      setHasCredits((p?.credits_remaining ?? 0) > 0);\\r\\n      return {\\r\\n        pass: p ? { credits_remaining: p.credits_remaining, session_length_minutes: p.session_length_minutes, total_credits: p.total_credits, status: p.status } : null,\\r\\n        bookings: d.bookings ?? value: ValidatedNewPassInput }\\r\\n  | { ok: false; error: \\"invalid_credits\\" | \\"invalid_session_length\\" | \\"invalid_expiry\\" } {\\r\\n  const totalCredits = Number(input.totalCredits);\\r\\n  if (!Number.isInteger(totalCredits) || totalCredits < 1 || totalCredits > 50) {\\r\\n    return { ok: false, error: \\"invalid_credits\\" };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const sessionLengthMinutes = Number(input.sessionLengthMinutes);\\r\\n  if (!Number.isInteger(sessionLengthMinutes) || sessionLengthMinutes < 15 || sessionLengthMinutes > 480) {\\r\\n    return { ok: false, error: \\"invalid_session_length\\" };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (input.expiresAt === null || input.expiresAt === undefined || input.expiresAt === \\"\\") {\\r\\n    return { ok: true, value: { totalCredits, sessionLengthMinutes, expiresAt: null } };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (typeof input.expiresAt !== \\"string\\") return { ok: false, error: \\"invalid_expiry\\" };\\r\\n  const expiryMs = Date.parse(input.expiresAt);\\r\\n  if (!Number.isFinite(expiryMs) || expiryMs <= Date.now()) return { ok: false, error: \\"invalid_expiry\\" };\\r\\n\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    ok: true,\\r\\n    value: { totalCredits, sessionLengthMinutes, expiresAt: new Date(expiryMs).toISOString() },\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"q":3,"result":"    const { data: passRow } = await supabase.rpc(\\"resolve_booking_link_full\\", { _slug: slug });\\r\\n    // The resolver RETURNS booking_passes, so a non-resolving slug comes back as\\r\\n    // an all-NULL composite, not an empty result. ## assistant

Creating a booking pass grants sessions immediately and returns a private `/book/{slug}` link that works without an account. A matching student account also sees its balance at `/booking`; a name-only pass does not appear there until linked. The defaults are five 70-minute sessions with no expiration. For an existing account, creating a new pass replaces its active pass rather than adding sessions. These are code findings, not a live test.
